# Vorkerland decisions: keep visibility, price and timing at the UI boundary.
# Navigation:
# allied_support_decisions
# collapse_decisions
# diplomacy_decisions
# focus_operations_decisions (including postwar reclamation and integration)
# rom_tru_decisions

# --- allied_support_decisions ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_allied_support_category = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_send_vla_support_tranche_1 = {
		icon = ADISCORD_arms_supply
		allowed = { tag = WKR }
		visible = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_tranche_1_sent }
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted
			VLA = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				has_war = yes
				OR = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT is_subject_of = ROOT }
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted
			VLA = { exists = yes NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war = yes OR = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			has_manpower > 2999
			has_political_power > 49
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 15000 } }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < 150 } }
		}
		cost = 25
		days_remove = 5
		fire_only_once = no
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_send_vla_support_tranche_1_description_rules_tt
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_send_vla_support_tranche_1 = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 500 modifier = { factor = 2 VLA = { surrender_progress > 0.20 } } }
	}

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_send_vla_support_tranche_2 = {
		icon = ADISCORD_arms_supply
		allowed = { tag = WKR }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_tranche_1_sent
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_tranche_2_sent }

			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_cooldown }

			has_political_power > 49

		}

	}

# --- collapse_decisions ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_category = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_emergency_levies = {
		icon = ADISCORD_recruitment
		allowed = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_minor_combatant = yes }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
		}
		available = {
			has_war = yes
			capital_scope = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		cost = 25
		days_remove = 21
		fire_only_once = yes
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_emergency_levies_description_rules_tt
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_emergency_levies = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 250
			modifier = { factor = 3 num_divisions < 6 }
		}
	}

	# Northern fighting unlocks a visible industrial mobilisation, not an unseen
	# war timer. The invasion mission begins only after this 120-day preparation
	# still satisfies the material readiness contract.
	ADISCORD_ivanland_prepare_northern_expedition = {
		icon = ADISCORD_mobilization
		allowed = { tag = IVN }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_planning_unlocked_v4
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_stood_down_v4 }
		}
		available = { ADISCORD_ivanland_intervention_start_ready = yes }
		cost = 75
		days_remove = 120
		fire_only_once = yes
		modifier = { civilian_factory_use = 2 }
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_ivanland_prepare_northern_expedition_description_rules_tt
			if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_ivanland_intervention_start_ready = yes }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_activate_ivanland_intervention_mission = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_stood_down_v4
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.80 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 200
			modifier = { factor = 0 ADISCORD_ivanland_intervention_start_ready = no }
			modifier = { factor = 2 num_divisions > 17 has_war_support > 0.50 }
		}
	}

	# One shared decision opens every still-valid peripheral rivalry after a
	# one-day diplomatic-cache barrier. The north is a real three-sided campaign:
	# Zaozersk, Norven and Grain all fight one another in separate war objects.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_regional_fronts = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { OR = { tag = ZAO tag = VLA tag = ROM tag = SOL tag = TRU } }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claim_wars_authorized
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began
				}
			}
		}
		available = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tag = ZAO
					WPA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
					WPS = { exists = yes is_subject = no }
					PSD = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
					PWR = { exists = yes is_subject = no }
				}
				AND = {
					tag = VLA
					TGD = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
					EBA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				AND = { tag = ROM DVA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
				AND = { tag = SOL SRA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } CSL = { exists = yes is_subject = no } }
				AND = { tag = TRU ZTA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_regional_war_factions = yes
			# leave_faction/dismantle_faction reaches the diplomatic cache on the
			# following day. The hidden event opens every still-valid rivalry once.
			# there is no daily or monthly watchdog.
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.63 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 1000 }
	}

	# The only route by which a regional or district winner reaches the central
	# war. It is gated on the local bracket actually being cleared, and it splices
	# the winner into the host's existing war through faction membership plus
	# add_to_war rather than opening a second war object. One targeted decision
	# covers all three claimants instead of a copied decision per host.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_claimant_coalition = {
		icon = ADISCORD_negotiations
		allowed = {
			NOT = { OR = { tag = ROM tag = TRU } }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_bracket_member = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_regional_combatant = yes
			}
		}
		# Only the three claimants are ever candidate hosts, so the target list is
		# closed and the expensive host trigger runs at most three times. The root
		# gate keeps it from running at all outside the collapse window.
		targets = { WKR VAD TVA }
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid }
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			is_subject = no
			is_in_faction = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { OR = { tag = ROM tag = TRU } }
			has_war = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			capital_scope = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_local_rival = no
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_coalition_host_for_ROOT = yes }
		}
		cost = 25

		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 7
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_claimant_coalition_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_coalition_of_FROM = yes
			}
		}
		# No claimant is excluded by tag: districts drift toward whoever holds the
		# fewest central districts, so the coalition evens the war out instead of
		# piling onto one side.
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 200
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received FROM = { tag = WKR } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_local_rival = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_control_leader = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_control_laggard = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 num_divisions > 8 }
		}
	}

	# A victorious and independent Frealor may open a limited northern operation.
	# It receives the corridor only after physically controlling all three states.
	# a timeout or the end of the central war closes both wars without transfers.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention = {
		icon = ADISCORD_operations
		allowed = { tag = ROM }
		# Keep this player/AI-selected mission from auto-starting on a transient
		# availability tick. its click remains the only 240-day timer authority.
		activation = { always = no }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_start_ready = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved }
		}
		available = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_start_ready = yes
			is_subject = no
			has_war = no
			ZAO = { exists = yes is_subject = no controls_state = 72 }
			WPS = { exists = yes is_subject = no controls_state = 196 controls_state = 322 }
		}
		cost = 50
		selectable_mission = yes
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 240
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_description_rules_tt
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_active
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_succeeded
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failed
			declare_war_on = {
				target = ZAO
				type = take_state_focus
				generator = { 72 }
			}
			declare_war_on = {
				target = WPS
				type = take_state_focus
				generator = { 196 322 }
			}
			# The mission UI is not the lifecycle authority. A one-day assertion catches
			# rejected declarations, while the bounded watchdog guarantees a terminal
			# outcome even if Clausewitz drops the selectable-mission timeout effect.
			set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_watchdog_scheduled days = 241 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.46 days = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.45 days = 240 }
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { controls_state = 72 controls_state = 196 controls_state = 322 }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { controls_state = 72 controls_state = 196 controls_state = 322 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_success = yes
			}
			else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failure = yes }
		}
		timeout_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_rom_northern_intervention_timeout = yes }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 225 }
	}

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_btl_support_wrk = {

		allowed = { tag = BTL }

		}

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_continue_reunification = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			NOT = { tag = WRK }
		}
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier
			any_neighbor_country = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_reunification_target_for_ROOT = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}
		available = {
			any_neighbor_country = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_reunification_target_for_ROOT = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 7
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_continue_reunification_description_rules_tt
			every_neighbor_country = {
				limit = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_reunification_target_for_ROOT = yes
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				}
				ROOT = { declare_war_on = { target = PREV type = annex_everything } }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 250
			modifier = { factor = 0 tag = VAD }
		}
	}

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_macri_piv_pact = {
		icon = ADISCORD_negotiations
		allowed = { tag = EBA }
		visible = {
			controls_state = 74
			controls_state = 105
			controls_state = 197
			NOT = { country_exists = VLA }
			NOT = { country_exists = TGD }
			PIV = { exists = yes is_in_faction = no is_neighbor_of = ROOT }
			is_in_faction = no
		}
		available = { has_war = no }
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = faction_ebern_afrela_compact
			}
			add_to_faction = PIV
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_macri_allied_piv
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 300 }
	}

	# Zaozersk is the only northern administration that may return to WRK.
	# Volnograd keeps its separate western loyalist route. technocratic Rimat does
	# not receive this decision at all.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_loyalist_district = {
		icon = ADISCORD_state_directive
		allowed = { OR = { tag = ZAO tag = VLA } }
		visible = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_loyalist_district_restored }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				exists = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_postwar_integration
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified
				owns_state = 32
				controls_state = 32
			}
			OR = {
				AND = { tag = ZAO NOT = { country_exists = WPA } NOT = { country_exists = WPS } }
				AND = { tag = VLA NOT = { country_exists = EBA } NOT = { country_exists = TGD } }
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_war = no
			is_subject = no
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_postwar_integration
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified
				owns_state = 32
				controls_state = 32
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = ROOT
					autonomy_state = autonomy_district_in_Vorkerland
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_loyalist_district_restored
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 350 }
	}

	# Both client administrations are postwar creations. Ivanland first has to
	# occupy all four settlement states. IBA is then created before IBL.
	ADISCORD_ivanland_limited_intervention = {
		icon = ADISCORD_operations
		allowed = { tag = IVN }
		# Non-selectable missions otherwise auto-activate from their `activation`
		# trigger. Keep it permanently false so the post-war event's
		# `activate_mission` call is the only authority for the 240-day deadline.
		activation = { always = no }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
		}
		# The preparation decision activates this mission directly. `always = no`
		# prevents a second, player-clicked deadline from diverging from the one
		# authoritative 240-day timer.
		available = { always = no }
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 240
		is_good = yes
		cancel_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
			OR = {
				AND = {
					controls_state = 90
					controls_state = 91
					controls_state = 93
					controls_state = 94
				}
				has_capitulated = yes
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					controls_state = 90
					controls_state = 91
					controls_state = 93
					controls_state = 94
				}
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_success = yes
			}
			else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes }
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_ivanland_limited_intervention_description_rules_tt
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 100 }
	}

	# If the claimant war has exhausted every surviving principal, Ivanland can
	# exploit the deadlock. TVA is checked only while it still exists. its prior
	# destruction therefore cannot block the second intervention.
	ADISCORD_ivanland_second_intervention = {
		icon = ADISCORD_operations
		allowed = { tag = IVN }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_second_intervention_started }
			PSD = { exists = yes }
			PWR = { exists = yes }
		}
		available = {
			has_war = no
			WKR = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = VAD
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
					value = 100
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			VAD = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = WKR
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
					value = 100
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { country_exists = TVA }
				TVA = {
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
						value = 100
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
			PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
			PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
		}
		cost = 75
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_ivanland_second_intervention_description_rules_tt
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_second_intervention_started
			declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything }
			declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything }
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 350 }
	}

	# The worker claimant's two offshore garrisons can be isolated and occupied without opening
	# another full war. This is a one-time political settlement, not a recurring
	# state-repair pulse.
	ADISCORD_ivanland_occupy_wrk_islands = {
		icon = generic_naval
		allowed = { tag = IVN }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_wrk_islands_occupied }
			WKR = { exists = yes }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = WKR }
			IIA = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject_of = IVN
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			200 = { is_owned_by = WKR is_controlled_by = WKR }
			201 = { is_owned_by = WKR is_controlled_by = WKR }
		}
		cost = 50
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_ivanland_occupy_wrk_islands_description_rules_tt
			IIA = {
				transfer_state = 200
				transfer_state = 201
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			200 = { set_state_controller_to = IIA }
			201 = { set_state_controller_to = IIA }
			WKR = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_wrk_islands_occupied
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 200 }
	}

	# This offer exists only while the claimant war is unresolved. Once taken,
	# Workerland's post-war 50/50 restoration-or-war settlement is suppressed.
	ADISCORD_ivanland_guarantee_free_republics = {

		allowed = { tag = IVN }

		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_ivanland_guarantee_free_republics_description_rules_tt
			diplomatic_relation = { country = ROM relation = guarantee active = yes }
			diplomatic_relation = { country = TRU relation = guarantee active = yes }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_guaranteed_by_ivn
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 250 }
	}

	ADISCORD_ivanland_form_norvane_alliance = {

# --- court intrigue decisions ---
# Approaches and the dossier share one slot: the Tower's flag WRK_intrigue_work
# holds it from payment until the work ends or the collapse cuts it short.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_court_category = {

	WRK_intrigue_approach_1 = {
		icon = generic_political_discourse
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes
			WRK_intrigue_active = yes
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_intrigue_work_free_tt
				40 = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work } }
			}
		}
		cost = 35
		days_remove = 21
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_intrigue_work_begin_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				40 = {
					set_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work
					set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_work_paid value = 35 }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = { WRK_intrigue_active = yes }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				40 = { WRK_intrigue_refund_work = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			40 = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_intrigue_approach_1_tt
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_collect_officer_returns } }
					custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_intrigue_returns_bonus_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work }
						clr_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work
						clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_work_paid
						WRK_intrigue_set_approach_delta = yes
						WRK_intrigue_change_1 = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				40 = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_1 value = 64 compare = greater_than } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power < 90
			}
		}
	}

	# --- capital cells ---
	# Operations of the engineers' branch on the eight capital districts. Planting
	# and expanding share one slot: the district flag WRK_cell_work holds it from
	# payment until the work ends or is cancelled.

	WRK_cell_plant = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 32 33 34 35 36 37 38 39 }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM }
		}
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes
			WRK_cells_active = yes
			FROM = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 compare = less_than } }
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_cells_work_free_tt
				WRK_cells_work_free = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 30
		days_remove = 21
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_work_begin_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					set_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
					set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_work_paid value = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
		# The collapse ends the network's ledger; work cut short returns its price.
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { WRK_cells_active = yes }
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { WRK_cell_refund_work = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_plant_result_tt
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_reopen_emergency_workshops } }
					custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_plant_cover_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_work }
						clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
						clear_variable = WRK_cell_work_paid
						set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 }
						set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = 10 }
						if = {
							limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_reopen_emergency_workshops } }
							set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = 5 }
						}
						WRK_cell_heat_change = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power < 90
			}
		}
	}

	WRK_cell_expand = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 32 33 34 35 36 37 38 39 }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM }
		}
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes
			has_completed_focus = WRK_worx_network_register
			WRK_cells_active = yes
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 3 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal }
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_cells_work_free_tt
				WRK_cells_work_free = yes
			}
			FROM = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = WRK_cell_not_exposed_tt
					NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = WRK_cell_not_dormant_tt
					NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_dormant }
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 40
		days_remove = 28
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_work_begin_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					set_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
					set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_work_paid value = 40 }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { WRK_cells_active = yes }
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { WRK_cell_refund_work = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_cell_expand_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					# A cell exposed or combed out during the work cannot grow: the price returns.
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
							check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than }
							NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
						}
						clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
						clear_variable = WRK_cell_work_paid
						add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 }
						clamp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level min = 0 max = 3 }
						set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = 15 }
						WRK_cell_heat_change = yes
					}
					else = { WRK_cell_refund_work = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power < 90
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 50 compare = greater_than } }
			}
		}
	}

			ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_open = yes

				NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project }
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_slot_tt
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_slot_free = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project_tt
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project_available = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_cost_45_2700_3000_0_0_0
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 45 }
			NOT = {
				has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 2700 }
			}
			NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
		}
		days_remove = 14
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_local_guard_description_rules_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_begin_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_local_guard_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project_available = yes
						has_completed_focus = WRK_contract_local_garrisons
						NOT = { has_political_power < 45 }
						NOT = {
							has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 2700 }
						}
						NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
					}
					add_political_power = -45
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
						type = infantry_equipment
						amount = -2700
					}
					add_manpower = -3000
					add_to_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_active
						value = 1
					}
					FROM = {
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
							value = 1
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
							value = ROOT.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_pp
							value = 45
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_rifles
							value = 2700
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_people
							value = 3000
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_support
							value = 0
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_trains
							value = 0
						}
						set_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_command
							value = 0
						}
						set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_open = yes }
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				}
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_refund_project = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_finish_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_open = yes
						FROM = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
						}
					}
					FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_finish_project = yes }
				}
				else = {
					FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_refund_project = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
					value = 2
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}

				NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_has_solar_intervention_border = yes }

					OR = {
						AND = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra country_exists = SRA }
						AND = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl country_exists = CSL }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_cancel_vad_solar_intervention_reservation = yes }
		remove_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solar_intervention = yes }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 800 }
	}

				set_variable = { var = WKR_programme_deposit value = 60 }
				set_country_flag = WKR_pattern_contract_pending
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_country_flag = WKR_pattern_contract_pending
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_programmes_open = yes
					}
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = WKR_pattern_contract
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = industry
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
					idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_licensed_patterns
					days = 70
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = 100
					producer = ROOT
				}
				hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_clear_programme = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_pattern_contract_pending } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refund_programme = yes
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = WKR_pattern_contract_pending } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refund_programme = yes
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 4 }
	}

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_front_deadline = {
		icon = ADISCORD_operations
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK } }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_deadline_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
		}
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 240
		cancel_trigger = {
			AND = {
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr } NOT = { has_war_with = EYR } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc } NOT = { has_war_with = EGC } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv } NOT = { has_war_with = RIV } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev } NOT = { has_war_with = REV } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor } NOT = { has_war_with = YOR } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn } NOT = { has_war_with = NDN } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb } NOT = { has_war_with = SWB } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv } NOT = { has_war_with = VHV } }
				OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv } NOT = { has_war_with = OSV } }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_finish_central_minor_wave = yes }
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_front_deadline_description_rules_tt
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_resolve_central_minor_wave_deadline = yes
		}
	}

	# One operational order opens every viable adjacent front at once. The
	# recorded target set remains stable until the whole wave has ended.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave = {
		icon = ADISCORD_operations
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK } }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_adjacent_viable_central_minor = yes
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_target_tt
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_adjacent_viable_central_minor = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_recovery_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_recovery_cooldown }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_pending_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_deadline_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_deadline_active }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_peace_tt
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_war_with = EYR
						has_war_with = EGC
						has_war_with = RIV
						has_war_with = REV
						has_war_with = YOR
						has_war_with = NDN
						has_war_with = SWB
						has_war_with = VHV
						has_war_with = OSV
					}
				}
			}
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		cost = 5
		days_remove = 1
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_minor_wave_description_rules_tt
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_cleanup_central_minor_front = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_country = { tag = EYR }
					EYR = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_is_open_to_ROOT = yes
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								has_war_with = WKR
								has_war_with = VAD
								has_war_with = TVA
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr
				EYR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_country = { tag = EGC }
					EGC = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_is_open_to_ROOT = yes
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								has_war_with = WKR
								has_war_with = VAD
								has_war_with = TVA
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc
				EGC = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_country = { tag = RIV }
					RIV = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_is_open_to_ROOT = yes
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								has_war_with = WKR
								has_war_with = VAD
								has_war_with = TVA
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv
				RIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_country = { tag = REV }
					REV = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }

	# A conquered district becomes a core only after its former government has
	# disappeared and the claimant owns and controls the entire historical
	# package. These short civil-administration decisions are identical for all
	# three claimants and cannot grant claims on land they have not secured.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK } }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_integration_available = yes
			NOT = { country_exists = EYR }
			owns_state = 102 owns_state = 109 owns_state = 111 owns_state = 325
			OR = { 102 = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } } 109 = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } } 111 = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } } 325 = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } } }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { country_exists = EYR }
			owns_state = 102 controls_state = 102
			owns_state = 109 controls_state = 109
			owns_state = 111 controls_state = 111
			owns_state = 325 controls_state = 325
		}
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_description_rules_tt
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_paid }
		}
		cost = 15
		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 7
		fire_only_once = no
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_paid }
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_paid
				custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_central_district_tt
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { country_exists = EYR } owns_state = 102 controls_state = 102 owns_state = 109 controls_state = 109 owns_state = 111 controls_state = 111 owns_state = 325 controls_state = 325 }
					hidden_effect = { 102 = { add_core_of = ROOT } 109 = { add_core_of = ROOT } 111 = { add_core_of = ROOT } 325 = { add_core_of = ROOT } }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
				}
				if = { limit = { NOT = { NOT = { country_exists = EYR }
							owns_state = 102 controls_state = 102
							owns_state = 109 controls_state = 109
							owns_state = 111 controls_state = 111
							owns_state = 325 controls_state = 325 } } add_political_power = 15 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 600 }
	}

	# Worker-route diplomacy belongs to the postwar focus category. Keeping this
	# here prevents the finished-collapse category from hiding the focus reward.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_recognize_free_republics = {
		icon = ADISCORD_negotiations
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_worker_free_republics
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_recognized }
			OR = {
				ROM = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				TRU = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		available = { has_war = no }
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROM = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
					NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROM }
				}
				diplomatic_relation = { country = ROM relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					TRU = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
					NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = TRU }
				}
				diplomatic_relation = { country = TRU relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_recognized
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_west_free_republics_lean_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_west_push value = 30 }
				ROM = { ADISCORD_west_push_lean = yes }
				TRU = { ADISCORD_west_push_lean = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 300 }
	}

	# District reconstruction. The paid programme reserves one concurrent slot
	# and its district from payment through completion or cancellation.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_district = {
		icon = ADISCORD_construction
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		visible = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes }
		state_target = yes
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		targets = { 27 32 33 34 35 36 37 38 39 40 75 79 81 82 102 104 105 106 107 108 109 110 111 121 122 123 124 198 200 201 202 306 307 308 309 311 315 316 317 318 320 323 324 325 327 }
		target_root_trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_reconstruction_open = yes }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_site = yes }
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_pw_rebuild_slot_tt
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_slot_free = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 40
		days_remove = 60
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_pw_rebuild_start_tt
			effect_tooltip = {
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_reward = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_begin_rebuild = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_finish_rebuild = yes }
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes }
				FROM = { NOT = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_release_rebuild = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = infrastructure
						size > 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

WRK_worx_research_category = {
	WRK_worx_basic = {
		icon = ADISCORD_science
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		visible = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes
			WRK_worx_mandate = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised }
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_worx_operational_tt
				WRK_worx_active = yes
			}
			WRK_worx_slot_free = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WRK_worx_no_emergency_tt
				NOT = { has_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = WRK_worx_basic_price
		custom_cost_trigger = { WRK_worx_basic_affordable = yes }
		days_remove = 14
		fire_only_once = no
		cancel_trigger = { hidden_trigger = { WRK_worx_active = no } }
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_basic_description_rules_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_basic_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						WRK_worx_active = yes
						WRK_worx_slot_free = yes
						NOT = { has_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days }
						WRK_worx_basic_affordable = yes
					}
					set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_receipt value = WRK_worx_basic_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_payment value = WRK_worx_receipt }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_payment value = -1 }
					add_political_power = WRK_worx_payment
					set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = { hidden_effect = { WRK_worx_finish_basic = yes } }
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_cancel_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 1 compare = equals } }
					WRK_worx_refund = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 35 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}

					}
				}
			}

		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_worx_cancel_tt
			hidden_effect = {

					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = WRK_worx_operation
							value = 17
							compare = equals
						}
					}
					WRK_worx_refund = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 10 }

# --- world_empire_decisions ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_category = {
	# One governor per former district state. The focus choice between
	# military and civil governors decides the installed administration.
	WRK_empire_appoint_governor = {
		icon = ADISCORD_deal
		allowed = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } }
		visible = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_not_wartime_claimant = yes }
		state_target = yes
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		targets = { 27 32 33 34 35 36 37 38 39 40 75 79 81 82 102 104 105 106 107 108 109 110 111 121 122 123 124 198 200 201 202 306 307 308 309 311 315 316 317 318 320 323 324 325 327 }
		target_root_trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_imperial_governors
				has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_officer_estates
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_active = yes
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_empire_governor_seat }
			}
		}
		highlight_states = { highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM } }
		available = {
			is_subject = no
			has_capitulated = no
		}
		cost = 50
		days_remove = 30
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_empire_appoint_governor_start_tt
			effect_tooltip = {
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_install_governor = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_active = yes
					is_subject = no
					has_capitulated = no
					FROM = {
						is_core_of = ROOT
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
						NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_empire_governor_seat }
					}
				}
				FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_install_governor = yes }
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_active = no
				is_subject = yes
				has_capitulated = yes
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
						NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power < 100
			}
		}
	}
}

						level = 1

						province = {
							all_provinces = yes
							limit_to_border = yes
						}
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						type = bunker
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
						province = {
							all_provinces = yes
							limit_to_victory_point = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = 50
					producer = WKR
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				surrender_progress > 0.20
			}
		}
	}

	WKR_measure_forward_depot_order = {
		icon = ADISCORD_logistics
		allowed = { tag = WKR }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 32 33 200 201 34 35 36 37 38 39 324 27 75 106 107 121 102 109 111 325 81 110 124 79 306 308 309 327 82 323 108 122 123 315 316 317 318 320 }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		visible = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			OR = {
				WKR_executive_course = yes
				has_completed_focus = WKR_empower_front_executive
			}
			has_completed_focus = WKR_measure_supply_hubs
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WKR_measure_forward_depot_site_tt
				FROM = { WKR_measure_forward_depot_site = yes }
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM }
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = WKR_measure_forward_depot_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = { WKR_measure_forward_depot_affordable = yes }
		days_remove = 21
		fire_only_once = no
		cancel_if_not_visible = yes
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				FROM = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measure_forward_depot } }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_measure_forward_depot_order_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_measure_depot_cancellation_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_political_power = -30
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = -40
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = -4
				}
				FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_measure_depot_in_progress }
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_measure_depot_in_progress }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment_1
				amount = 20
				producer = WKR
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_measure_depot_in_progress }
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
						supply_node > 0
						NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measure_forward_depot } }
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measure_forward_depot }
					hidden_effect = {
						add_to_array = { array = global.WKR_measure_forward_depot_states value = THIS }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { infrastructure < 5 }
						add_building_construction = {
							type = infrastructure
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = 40
					producer = WKR
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment_1
					amount = 4
					producer = WKR
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
	}

			hidden_effect = {
				add_command_power = -25
				add_manpower = -3000
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = -500
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			WKR_contract_fights_a_claimant = no
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			add_command_power = 25
			add_manpower = 3000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 500
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			army_experience = 20
			add_stability = -0.02
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_manpower < 10000
			}
		}
	}

	# Returned estimate: the ring closes around one enemy-held district for
	# forty-five days instead of a storm. One ring at a time: the country flag is
	# set at payment and cleared by the ring's end or cancellation, which also
	# removes the district modifier and returns the price.
	WKR_tally_ring_of_the_districts = {
		icon = generic_fortification
		allowed = { tag = WKR }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 32 33 34 35 36 37 38 39 }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { WKR_tally_ring_target = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			OR = {
				WKR_executive_course = yes
				has_completed_focus = WKR_empower_front_executive
			}
			has_completed_focus = WKR_tally_return_the_estimate
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WKR_contract_claimant_war_tt
				WKR_contract_fights_a_claimant = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = WKR_tally_ring_free_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_tally_ring_running }
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM }
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_trigger = { WKR_tally_ring_affordable = yes }
		custom_cost_text = WKR_tally_ring_cost
		days_remove = 45
		fire_only_once = no
		cancel_if_not_visible = yes
		modifier = {
			army_defence_factor = 0.05
			supply_consumption_factor = -0.05
		}
		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = WKR_tally_ringed_district
					days = 45
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_war_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_political_power = -20
				add_command_power = -15
				set_country_flag = WKR_tally_ring_running
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			WKR_contract_fights_a_claimant = no
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_tally_ringed_district } }
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_tally_ringed_district }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_tally_ring_running }
			add_political_power = 20
			add_command_power = 15
		}
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = WKR_tally_ring_running }
			army_experience = 15
			WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
	}
}
